		icon = GFX_SER_form_yugoslavia
		prerequisite = { focus = GRE_shall_rise_again }
		mutually_exclusive = { focus = European_Unity }
		mutually_exclusive = { focus = GRE_horror_and_fear }
		mutually_exclusive = { focus = GRE_macedonia_shall_rise }
		mutually_exclusive = { focus = GRE_path_to_rome }
		mutually_exclusive = { focus = GRE_the_ancient_hellinas }
		mutually_exclusive = { focus = GRE_the_right_to_rule }
		mutually_exclusive = { focus = GRE_befriend_our_moral_enemy }
		mutually_exclusive = { focus = GRE_resurrecting_the_megali_idea }

		x = -16
		y = 1
		relative_position_id = GRE_shall_rise_again
		cost = 5
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 1
		}
	allow_branch = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_game_rule = {
						rule = obsolete_focus_branches_visibility
						option = HIDE
					}
				}
				NOT = { has_completed_focus = European_Unity }
				NOT = { has_completed_focus = GRE_horror_and_fear }
				NOT = { has_completed_focus = GRE_macedonia_shall_rise }
				NOT = { has_completed_focus = GRE_path_to_rome }
				NOT = { has_completed_focus = GRE_the_ancient_hellinas }				
				NOT = { has_completed_focus = GRE_the_right_to_rule }	
				NOT = { has_completed_focus = GRE_befriend_our_moral_enemy }
				NOT = { has_completed_focus = GRE_resurrecting_the_megali_idea }	
			}
		}	
				offset = {
			x = 16
			y = 0
			trigger = {

					AND = {
						has_game_rule = {
							rule = obsolete_focus_branches_visibility
							option = HIDE
						}
						has_completed_focus = GRE_our_brothers_up_north
					}
				
			}
		}	
		completion_reward = {
			GRE = {
				diplomatic_relation = {
					country = YUG
					relation = guarantee
					active = yes
			   }
		 }	
			YUG = {
				diplomatic_relation = {
					country = GRE
					relation = guarantee
					active = yes
			   }
		 }			
			GRE = {
				diplomatic_relation = {
					country = YUG
					relation = non_aggression_pact
					active = yes
			   }
		 }	
	hidden_effect = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_game_rule = {
							rule = obsolete_focus_branches_visibility
							option = HIDE
						}
					}
					mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes
				}
			}
	}
}
